| 2026-08-07 | `claude/system/execution_prompt.md` | v3.64→v3.65 | Lessons-learnt deferred patch resolution (`2026-08-05__release-v8.3` Phase 3 friction item 2): §5.1 "Frontend testing gate (LL-v3.1-EX-01 — hard gate)" gains a new environment-parity sub-clause — a sandboxed/local pre-merge review pass is not a fully reliable predictor of real-CI Playwright outcomes for focus-restoration, focus-trap, and other interaction-timing behaviour specifically, confirmed at ST-11 (`Dialog.Content` focus-restoration assumption passed sandboxed review but failed real GitHub Actions CI, `SC-CR-11`). For ACs in this sub-class, DoQ sign-off comments must now record that the specific Playwright scenario was observed passing in a real GitHub Actions CI run (citing the run URL/commit SHA) — a locally-authored-and-reviewed test alone no longer satisfies step 1 of the gate. Authority: Base44 Frontend Prompt Owner (reviewing `2026-08-05__release-v8.3` lessons_learnt_closure.md deferred patches, resolved 2026-08-07). | Base44 Frontend Prompt Owner (2026-08-07) |
| 2026-08-07 | `claude/system/shared_standards.md` | v3.24→v3.25 | Lessons-learnt deferred patch resolution (`2026-08-05__release-v8.3` Phase 3 friction items 3-4): new §6.1 CI Failure Diagnosis and Workflow-Authoring Guidance. Documents `scripts/check_ci_infra_outage.py` (classifies a failed GitHub Actions run/job as infra-outage vs real by scanning job logs for known signature strings — `"Failed to resolve action download info"`, `"Error: Service Unavailable"`, cancelled-with-no-`startedAt` queue-timeout pattern — and detects the "stuck rerun" symptom where `gh run rerun` reports `"workflow file may be broken"` while the run is still queued/in-progress; read-only, recommends an empty retrigger commit on a fresh SHA rather than fighting a stuck run) and the `pipefail`/`tee` exit-code capture gap (GitHub Actions `run:` steps default to `bash -e {0}` with no `pipefail` unless `shell: bash` is declared, silently defeating `cmd \| tee file; echo $?` patterns — new steps must declare `shell: bash` or capture via `${PIPESTATUS[0]}`). Both root-caused during the 2026-08-06 GitHub Actions outage (PR #1259/#1260). Authority: Head of Engineering (reviewing `2026-08-05__release-v8.3` lessons_learnt_closure.md deferred patches, resolved 2026-08-07). | Head of Engineering (2026-08-07) |

| 2026-03-16 | `roadmap_prompt.md` | v2.8→v3.0 | Five governance improvements: (1) STEP 0.C auto-tier determination — system classifies runs as Lightweight/Standard/Extended from objective criteria; Lightweight uses single cycle_record.md for STEP 2–7 outputs. | 2026-07-06 | `claude/system/backlog_management_prompt.md` | v1.9→v1.10 | v6.6 ST-03 (BLG-QA-72, EPIC-02): STEP 4.5 ID Uniqueness Scan — added §6.1 stub+verbatim exemption. An ID appearing exactly twice, with matching title text, where the first occurrence is immediately followed by a `**Status at retirement:**` stub line, is the documented §6.1 archive format and must not be flagged as a duplicate. Only IDs appearing >2 times, or twice with differing titles, or twice without the stub marker, are genuine duplicates. Root cause: direct audit of backlog.md/backlog_archive.md found 39 IDs with >1 `###` header; 29 were compliant §6.1 pairs the prior scan wrongly flagged, making "0 unresolved duplicate IDs" structurally unreachable; 10 were genuine collisions (renumbered this commit); 5 were the same item archived twice under two historical conventions (flagged as a follow-up dedup backlog item, not renumbered). | 2026-07-06 | `claude/system/roadmap_prompt.md` | v8.2→v8.3 | Roadmap rebalance 2026-07-06__scheduled STEP 5 debate (`IDEA-challenger-20260702-01`, resolves `lessons_learnt_closure.md` v6.6 Carry-Forward #2 deferred patch): §7.1 — added "Mandatory pull-forward on sustained failure." After 3+ consecutive worsening/unresolved rolling-3-cycle Skill-Silo readings, the pull-forward recommendation becomes mandatory: PO must commit ≥2 build-and-ship-shaped U-items at the next release; audit/investigation-shaped stories (AC requires only findings/decision, no shipped user-visible change) do not count even if nominally labelled U at scoping, per the STEP 2.4 content-based test. Closes the gap where v6.5 and v6.6 each bundled 2 nominal U-items but only 1 resolved to genuine U at ship in both cases. Challenger raised a Park counter-argument (governance-complexity risk, citing GCA-2026-06-17); PO Modified scope to a single clause addition (no new subsystem) rather than a full second escalation tier, which resolved the counter-argument.
